				CANDIDATE = root
				CONSECRATION_PROVINCE = province:8 # Kirkwall, the cathedral of St Magnus built for his relics
				PRESET = yes
			}
			religion:christianity_religion = {
				every_faith = {
					limit = {
						MB_western_saint_faith_trigger = yes
						main_rite = { is_rite_enabled = yes }
						NOT = { has_saint_character = root }
					}
					add_saint = { character = root }
				}
			}
		}
	}
